- **feihua-literature**: Rewrite any article into Feihua Literature (廢話文學) — the 2021–2022 Chinese internet meme genre that produces grammatically perfect, tonally earnest sentences carrying zero new information. Hallmarks: synonym substitution (「聽君一席話，如聽一席話」), tautology / contradiction (「這個西瓜很甜，因為它不苦」), and Q-keyword echo (「Q: 你為什麼遲到？ A: 因為我沒有準時。」). The text must read as if it were earnestly delivering truth while in fact saying nothing. Primarily user-invoked via 「/feihua-literature」; also triggers on 「改寫成廢話文學」、「廢話風格」、 「同義反覆」、「套套邏輯」、「聽君一席話如聽一席話」、「nonsense rewrite」、「tautology talk」. - **feinai-dispatch**: Use when tasks exist in feinai for a spec and need to be executed. Dispatches subagents to claim and complete tasks, enforces worktree isolation, handles parallelism (sequential or parallel based on user choice), and resolves merge conflicts and failures by blocking the loop until repair completes. Replaces `subagent-driven-development` from superpowers when feinai is active. ([mvisca/feinai](https://github.com/mvisca/feinai/tree/HEAD/skills/feinai-dispatch) · 2 copies)
- **feinai-implement**: Use when a feinai task needs to be executed. Claims one pending task from feinai, implements it in an isolated worktree, runs quality gates, and pushes to main. Run standalone for a single task; `feinai-dispatch` uses its own subagent prompt and integrates by merge. ([mvisca/feinai](https://github.com/mvisca/feinai/tree/HEAD/skills/feinai-implement) · 2 copies)
- **feinai-sdd**: Use when working in spec-driven development (SDD) workflows that involve brainstorming, writing-plans, or subagent-driven-development AND the project has a `.feinai/feinai.db` file. Replaces creating markdown files in `docs/superpowers/specs/` and `docs/superpowers/plans/` with atomic `feinai` CLI calls, keeping state queryable, race-free, and audit-logged. Also use when the user asks to create a spec, add a task, claim work, or mark progress. ([mvisca/feinai](https://github.com/mvisca/feinai/tree/HEAD/skills/feinai-sdd) · 2 copies)
- **feinai-write-spec**: Use when the user wants to design a new feature, refactor, or change in a project that has `.feinai/feinai.db`. With no argument, runs the full pipeline (spec + plan + tasks). With a SPEC-ID argument, skips to task generation only (use when iterating on an existing plan). Replaces `brainstorming` + `writing-plans` from superpowers when feinai is active, and includes the `feinai-write-tasks` step. ([mvisca/feinai](https://github.com/mvisca/feinai/tree/HEAD/skills/feinai-write-spec) · 2 copies)
- **feinai-write-tasks**: Use when a spec + plan already exist in feinai (written by `feinai-write-spec` or manually) and need to be broken down into executable tasks. Decomposes the plan into atomic `feinai add` calls, analyzes file-level parallelism, and embeds TDD instructions. Output: a set of tasks with `blocked_by` dependencies ready for `feinai-dispatch` to execute. ([mvisca/feinai](https://github.com/mvisca/feinai/tree/HEAD/skills/feinai-write-tasks) · 2 copies)
- **feinman-codebase-guide**: Navigate, explain, trace execution flows, and plan code changes for feinman using Feinman's codebase intelligence. ([MrTheMech/feinman](https://github.com/MrTheMech/feinman/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/feinman) · ★1 · 2 copies)
